**Q: Tell me about PhishGuard.**
> PhishGuard is Ezhil's cybersecurity project. It's a real-time phishing email and spam classifier that uses supervised ML + NLP (TF-IDF, tokenization) to detect malicious emails, suspicious URLs, and fraud patterns. Key features: multi-feature analysis pipeline (email content + URL patterns + sender behaviour), end-to-end data pipeline, and a live threat alert module. Stack: Python, Scikit-learn, Pandas, TF-IDF. Pipeline: Email Input → NLP Parsing → ML Classifier → Threat Alert. GitHub: github.com/EZHILSAVIER/phishguard

**Q: Tell me about phishing email spam detection tool.**

> PhishGuard is Ezhil's cybersecurity project. It's a real-time phishing email and spam classifier that uses supervised ML + NLP (TF-IDF, tokenization) to detect malicious emails, suspicious URLs, and fraud patterns. Key features: multi-feature analysis pipeline (email content + URL patterns + sender behaviour), end-to-end data pipeline, and a live threat alert module. Stack: Python, Scikit-learn, Pandas, TF-IDF. Pipeline: Email Input → NLP Parsing → ML Classifier → Threat Alert. GitHub: github.com/EZHILSAVIER/phishguard

**Q: How does PhishGuard work?**
> 1. Raw email arrives
> 2. NLP pipeline parses content (TF-IDF vectorisation, tokenization)
> 3. ML classifier identifies threat patterns
> 4. Alert module notifies the user in real time
> It analyses three feature dimensions: email content, sender behaviour, and URL patterns.

**Q: Tell me about Sentient Shopper.**
> Sentient Shopper is an emotion-aware AI shopping assistant. It uses deep learning computer vision to detect a customer's facial emotion in real time, then a collaborative filtering + deep learning recommendation engine suggests personalised products based on that sentiment. Ezhil also ran EDA on customer behaviour data to improve recommendation accuracy. Pipeline: Face Scan → Emotion Label → Recommendation. Stack: Deep Learning, CV, Collaborative Filtering, Predictive Analytics.

**Q: Why emotion based recommendation in emotion AI shopping assistant?**
> Traditional recommendation systems ignore how a customer feels in the moment. Sentient Shopper bridges this gap — reading real-time emotion to deliver truly personalised shopping suggestions, boosting engagement and conversion.

**Q: How does Sentient Shopper work?**
> 1. Camera captures customer's face
> 2. Deep learning CV model classifies emotion (happy, sad, neutral, excited, etc.)
> 3. Emotion label feeds into the recommendation engine
> 4. Collaborative filtering + deep learning suggests relevant products
> 5. Data analytics on behaviour patterns continuously improve recommendations

**Q: Tell me about TrustCart.**
> TrustCart is Ezhil's most technically complex project. It's an AI-powered e-commerce compliance monitor that scrapes product listings, runs them through Gemini 2.5 Flash for AI analysis, uses OpenCV to detect watermark violations, and applies a weighted rule engine to flag Indian legal violations and generate risk reports. Stack: React 19 + FastAPI + MongoDB + Gemini 2.5 Flash + OpenCV + CloudScraper. Pipeline: URL Input → AI Scrape → Rule Engine → Risk Report. GitHub: github.com/EZHILSAVIER/trustcart

**Q: What problem does TrustCart solve?**
> Many e-commerce listings on Indian platforms violate consumer protection laws — missing mandatory information, using illegal pricing tactics, or lacking required disclosures. TrustCart automates compliance checks at scale, flagging violations before they become legal problems.

**Q: How does TrustCart work?**
> 1. User inputs a product listing URL
> 2. CloudScraper fetches the listing data
> 3. Gemini 2.5 Flash analyses content for compliance violations
> 4. OpenCV checks images for watermarks and image-level violations
> 5. Weighted rule engine scores and prioritises violations
> 6. Risk report is generated and displayed on the React 19 dashboard
